> - fragmented_row
> 
> CONTRACT SHAPE (HARD FAIL)
> - Must output OUTPUT A then OUTPUT B, or QA FAIL only.
> - Must not output narration.
> 
> TAB ENFORCEMENT (HARD FAIL)
> Let Ncols = 19 => expected_tabs = 18
> GATE_TSV_HEADER_TABS: header must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> GATE_TSV_ROW_TABS: each data row must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> GATE_SUMMARY_LINE_TABS: each summary line must contain exactly 1 TAB character.
> 
> TIER GATES (HARD FAIL)
> GATE_TIER_LABEL_SET: any non-blank tier must be in allowed set.
> GATE_TIER_ON_NON_REVOLVING: tier must be blank unless Account Type == REVOLVING.
> 
> QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> Reason\tDetails
> <gate_id>\tExpected=<expected>; Observed=<observed>; Sample=<sample_line>; Fix=<fix_hint>

> MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> 
> MISSION
> Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> 
> OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> 
> OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> 
> EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> 1) Start timer (ms).
> 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> 4) Compute fields.
> 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> 
> AUTHORITY FILES
> - SPEC_CORE.txt
> - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)

> Total Closed Accounts

> Grand Total Debt

> Sort Time (ms)
> Emit Time (ms)

> 
> KEY RULES

> - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).

> - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> - Allowed tier labels only:

>   Excellent (1–3%)
>   Low (4–7%)
>   Review (8–9%)
>   Medium (10%+)
>   Medium-High (20%+)
>   High (30%+)

> SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE

> # 【2†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> # 【5†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only
> # 【6†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".
> # 【7†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file9
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.

> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file10
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report TSV extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] OUTPUT (HARD)
> [L4] - Output ONLY the contract blocks:
> [L5]   OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY  (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L6]   OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L7]   OR output ONLY QA FAIL.
> [L8] - No narration, no markdown.
> [L9] 
> [L10] SPEED (HARD)
> [L11] - Parse once → sort once → emit.
> [L12] - Do NOT do multi-pass verification.
> [L13] - If unreadable/fragmented/scanned => FAIL FAST with QA FAIL.
> [L14] 
> [L15] TSV (HARD)
> [L16] - All tables MUST use literal TAB characters between columns.
> [L17] - Never space-align columns.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn1file12
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> # 【1†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> # 【2†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file2
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".

> # 【4†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-889aKgioqc2WDHFBCLf6ZU】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.3_CONTRACT_DEBUGGABLE
> [L3] Checklist Authority: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3
> [L4] 
> [L5] MISSION
> [L6] Produce contract-compliant, Google-Sheets-ready outputs:
> [L7] 
> [L8] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L9] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L10] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L11] 
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] - Summary (2 columns, TAB-separated)
> [L14] - TSV (TAB-separated; exact header)
> [L15] 
> [L16] FILE PRECEDENCE (HIGHEST→LOWEST)
> [L17] 1) SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> [L18] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt
> [L19] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L20] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L21] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> [L22] 
> [L23] EXECUTION FLOW (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L24] 1) Extract metadata:
> [L25]    - bureau
> [L26]    - report_date (as-of date) REQUIRED for age calculations
> [L27]    - fico score if present (summary only)
> [L28] 2) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only). Never include INQUIRY in tradeline TSV.
> [L29] 3) Normalize Account Type:
> [L30]    - REVOLVING (credit card/LOC)
> [L31]    - INSTALLMENT/LOAN
> [L32]    - DEPOSIT/OTHER (e.g., overdraft/reserve checking)
> [L33] 4) Compute derived fields per SPEC_CORE.
> [L34] 5) Build Output A (Open only) and Output B (Open+Closed).
> [L35] 6) Sort within each output by Opening Date oldest→newest; missing/unparseable last (stable).
> [L36] 7) Build Summary blocks (2 columns, tab-separated) per SPEC_CORE required keys.
> [L37] 8) Run QA gates (including reconciliation gates). If any fail => QA FAIL with details + fix hints.
> [L38] 9) Output:
> [L39]    - PASS => Output A block then Output B block (no extra text)
> [L40]    - FAIL => QA FAIL block only
> # 【5†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.
> # 【6†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn2file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> # 【0†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-UdH7PZmV8pCLCqJ554PU6J】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] Loan flags:
> [L2] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L3] 
> [L4] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L5] DTI
> [L6] Strategic Notes
> [L7] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L8] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L9] 
> [L10] ====================
> [L11] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L12] ====================
> [L13] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L14] 
> [L15] Rules:
> [L16] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L17] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - As-of date = report_date
> [L23] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L24] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L25] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L26] 
> [L27] ====================
> [L28] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L29] ====================
> [L30] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L31] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L32] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L33] 
> [L34] Utilization %:
> [L35] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.
> [L36] 
> [L37] Allowed tier labels (EXACT; no numeric ranges like "1–9%" allowed):
> [L38] Excellent (0%)
> [L39] Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40] Low (4–7%)
> [L41] Review (8–9%)
> [L42] Medium (10%+)
> [L43] Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44] High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] ====================
> [L47] F) ACCOUNT TYPE NORMALIZATION
> [L48] ====================
> [L49] REVOLVING: credit cards, bankcards, LOC.
> [L50] INSTALLMENT/LOAN: student/auto/personal/mortgage loans.
> [L51] DEPOSIT/OTHER: overdraft / reserve checking accounts and similar.
> [L52] 
> [L53] If unclear: blank + AMBIGUOUS_ACCOUNT_TYPE
> [L54] 
> [L55] ====================
> [L56] G) LOAN REMAINING FLAG (LOANS ONLY)
> [L57] ====================
> [L58] Remaining % = Balance / Original Balance (only if Original Balance present and >0)
> [L59] If Remaining % >= 85%: add LOAN_HIGH_REMAINING_85PCT
> [L60] Never apply to REVOLVING or DEPOSIT/OTHER.
> [L61] 
> [L62] ====================
> [L63] H) SORTING (REQUIRED)
> [L64] ====================
> [L65] Sort by Opening Date oldest→newest for parseable dates.
> [L66] Missing/unparseable dates go last (stable) and add OPEN_DATE_UNPARSEABLE.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] OUTPUT CONTRACT (HARD)
> [L4] - Output ONLY the contract blocks (Output A then Output B), OR output ONLY a QA FAIL block.
> [L5] - Never output narration, markdown, or explanations outside QA FAIL.
> [L6] 
> [L7] DEBUGGABILITY (ON FAIL)
> [L8] - When failing, QA FAIL must include full actionable details:
> [L9]   - which gate failed
> [L10]   - which rows/fields triggered it (as identifiers)
> [L11]   - expected vs observed
> [L12]   - a concrete fix hint
> [L13] 
> [L14] PERFORMANCE
> [L15] - No long deliberation loops.
> [L16] - If scanned/unreadable/fragmented => FAIL FAST (<10s) with QA FAIL.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file8
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file10
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.3 (Contract + Audit-Friendly)
> [L2] 
> [L3] ====================
> [L4] A) OUTPUT BLOCK FORMAT (EXACT)
> [L5] ====================
> [L6] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L7] <Summary A: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L8] <blank line>
> [L9] <TSV A: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L10] 
> [L11] <blank line>
> [L12] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L13] <Summary B: 2 columns, TAB-separated; one key per line>
> [L14] <blank line>
> [L15] <TSV B: header row + rows, TAB-separated>
> [L16] 
> [L17] No other text is allowed.
> [L18] 
> [L19] ====================
> [L20] B) SUMMARY (2 COLUMNS, TAB-SEPARATED; REQUIRED KEYS)
> [L21] ====================
> [L22] - Each summary line MUST be: <Key>\t<Value>
> [L23] - No header row like "Summary Key Value" (forbidden).
> [L24] 
> [L25] Bureau label (TU):
> [L26] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN ONLY)
> [L27] - TRANSUNION SUMMARY (OPEN + CLOSED)
> [L28] 
> [L29] Required keys (appear exactly once; blank value allowed):
> [L30] Report Date
> [L31] Bureau
> [L32] FICO Score 8
> [L33] 
> [L34] Counts:
> [L35] Total Open Accounts
> [L36] Total Closed Accounts
> [L37] Total Accounts
> [L38] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L39] 
> [L40] Ages:
> [L41] Average Account Age (yrs decimal)   (4dp)
> [L42] Average Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L43] Oldest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L44] Newest Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L45] 
> [L46] Debt totals (numeric or blank):
> [L47] Grand Total Debt
> [L48] Total Revolving Debt
> [L49] Total Auto Loan Debt
> [L50] Total Personal Loan Debt
> [L51] Total Student Loan Debt
> [L52] Total Mortgage Debt
> [L53] Total Other Debt
> [L54] 
> [L55] Revolving aggregates:
> [L56] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L57] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L58] 
> [L59] Tier counts (ONLY for REVOLVING; ordered low-risk → high-risk):
> [L60] Tier Count — Excellent (0%)
> [L61] Tier Count — Excellent (1–3%)
> [L62] Tier Count — Low (4–7%)
> [L63] Tier Count — Review (8–9%)
> [L64] Tier Count — Medium (10%+)
> [L65] Tier Count — Medium-High (20%+)
> [L66] Tier Count — High (30%+)
> [L67] 
> [L68] Loan flags:
> [L69] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L70] 
> [L71] Explicit exclusions (MUST NOT appear):
> [L72] DTI
> [L73] Strategic Notes
> [L74] Total Account Age (yrs)
> [L75] Total Account Age (yrs+mos)
> [L76] 
> [L77] ====================
> [L78] C) TSV SCHEMA (ORDERED, EXACT)
> [L79] ====================
> [L80] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L81] 
> [L82] Rules:
> [L83] - Age (yrs+mos) MUST NOT be a TSV column.
> [L84] - Underwriting Notes / Additional Notes blank unless explicitly present.
> [L85] 
> [L86] ====================
> [L87] D) AGE (yrs decimal) — REQUIRED WHEN POSSIBLE
> [L88] ====================
> [L89] - As-of date = report_date
> [L90] - Compute elapsed FULL MONTHS between Opening Date and report_date.
> [L91] - Age (yrs decimal) = months/12 rounded to EXACTLY 4 decimals.
> [L92] - If missing/unparseable dates => Age blank; add AGE_NOT_COMPUTED.
> [L93] 
> [L94] ====================
> [L95] E) UTILIZATION — REVOLVING ONLY (STRICT)
> [L96] ====================
> [L97] - Compute Utilization % and Tier ONLY if Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L98] - For DEPOSIT/OTHER (including overdraft/reserve checking): utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L99] - For INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields MUST be blank.
> [L100] 
> [L101] Utilization %:
> [L102] Balance / Credit Limit * 100, round 2 decimals.

> # 【12†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-CrkACVyC7oc2ktmtAoMZh5】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file12
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.3 (HARD FAIL ONLY + CONTRACT GATE + RECONCILIATION)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT OUTPUT GATE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L9] (1) Missing either block header:
> [L10]     OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L11]     OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] (2) Missing Summary or TSV in either block.
> [L13] (3) TSV header mismatch (must match SPEC_CORE exactly).
> [L14] (4) Summary is not 2-column TAB-separated (e.g., contains "Summary Key Value").
> [L15] (5) Any INQUIRY rows appear inside TSV tables.
> [L16] (6) Any text outside contract blocks (narration leakage).
> [L17] 
> [L18] FIELD FORMAT GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L19] (7) Any non-blank Age (yrs decimal) is not exactly 4 decimals.
> [L20] (8) Any utilization tier appears on non-REVOLVING rows.
> [L21] (9) Any utilization tier label not in the allowed set.
> [L22] (10) Any DEPOSIT/OTHER row has utilization % or tier non-blank.
> [L23] 
> [L24] RECONCILIATION GATES (HARD FAIL; DEBUGGABLE)
> [L25] Definitions:
> [L26] - A_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output A.
> [L27] - B_row_count = number of TSV rows in Output B.
> [L28] - A_open_count_from_rows = count(Open/Closed == Open) in Output A.
> [L29] - B_open_count_from_rows / B_closed_count_from_rows similarly.
> [L30] - Debt totals computed from TSV rows (numeric parse; blanks ignored).
> [L31] 
> [L32] Fail if ANY are true:
> [L33] (11) Summary key "Accounts Included (This Output)" != row_count for that output.
> [L34] (12) For Output A: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != A_row_count.
> [L35] (13) For Output B: Summary "Total Accounts" != B_row_count.
> [L36] (14) For Output B: Summary "Total Open Accounts" != B_open_count_from_rows.
> [L37] (15) For Output B: Summary "Total Closed Accounts" != B_closed_count_from_rows.
> [L38] (16) Summary "Grand Total Debt" != sum of balances across rows (by numeric parsing).
> [L39] (17) Summary "Total Revolving Debt" != sum of balances where Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L40] (18) Tier counts sum != number of REVOLVING rows with computable utilization.
> [L41] 
> [L42] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L43] Format:
> [L44] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L45] Reason\tDetails
> [L46] <reason>\t<details>
> [L47] 
> [L48] DEBUG DETAIL REQUIREMENT
> [L49] - Details MUST include: gate_id, expected, observed, and a fix_hint.
> [L50] - If a row triggers a gate, include a row identifier: "<Creditor Name>|<Opening Date>|<Open/Closed>".
> # 【13†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-8z5g4NR1KfZfr5ngPX3cmu】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn3file13
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.5 (MINIMAL + TSV ENFORCEMENT)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT SHAPE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] - Must output OUTPUT A then OUTPUT B, or QA FAIL only.
> [L9] - Must not output narration.
> [L10] 
> [L11] TAB ENFORCEMENT (HARD FAIL)
> [L12] Let Ncols = 19 => expected_tabs = 18
> [L13] GATE_TSV_HEADER_TABS: header must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L14] GATE_TSV_ROW_TABS: each data row must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L15] GATE_SUMMARY_LINE_TABS: each summary line must contain exactly 1 TAB character.
> [L16] 
> [L17] TIER GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L18] GATE_TIER_LABEL_SET: any non-blank tier must be in allowed set.
> [L19] GATE_TIER_ON_NON_REVOLVING: tier must be blank unless Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L20] 
> [L21] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L22] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L23] Reason\tDetails
> [L24] <gate_id>\tExpected=<expected>; Observed=<observed>; Sample=<sample_line>; Fix=<fix_hint>

> # 【0†SPEC_CORE.txt†file-5nVtnvQRm2rq4C3aaX1eYm】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file0
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.

> # 【3†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file3
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> # 【4†MASTER_INDEX.txt†file-Anne3UGRKfcD8AseR9foUH】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L9] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L13] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L14] 
> [L15] EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L16] 1) Start timer (ms).
> [L17] 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> [L18] 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> [L19] 4) Compute fields.
> [L20] 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> [L21] 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> [L22] 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> [L23] 
> [L24] AUTHORITY FILES
> [L25] - SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L26] - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L27] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt
> # 【5†QA_AND_GATES.txt†file-8z5g4NR1KfZfr5ngPX3cmu】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: QA_AND_GATES.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file5
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] QA AND GATES — v2.5 (MINIMAL + TSV ENFORCEMENT)
> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page
> [L5] - fragmented_row
> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT SHAPE (HARD FAIL)
> [L8] - Must output OUTPUT A then OUTPUT B, or QA FAIL only.
> [L9] - Must not output narration.
> [L10] 
> [L11] TAB ENFORCEMENT (HARD FAIL)
> [L12] Let Ncols = 19 => expected_tabs = 18
> [L13] GATE_TSV_HEADER_TABS: header must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L14] GATE_TSV_ROW_TABS: each data row must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L15] GATE_SUMMARY_LINE_TABS: each summary line must contain exactly 1 TAB character.
> [L16] 
> [L17] TIER GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L18] GATE_TIER_LABEL_SET: any non-blank tier must be in allowed set.
> [L19] GATE_TIER_ON_NON_REVOLVING: tier must be blank unless Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L20] 
> [L21] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L22] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L23] Reason\tDetails
> [L24] <gate_id>\tExpected=<expected>; Observed=<observed>; Sample=<sample_line>; Fix=<fix_hint>
> # 【6†SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt†file-TrmDU3EQSp5dzwC3yj25pi】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SYSTEM_INSTRUCTIONS.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn4file6
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] You are a silent, underwriting-grade credit report TSV extractor.
> [L2] 
> [L3] OUTPUT (HARD)
> [L4] - Output ONLY the contract blocks:
> [L5]   OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY  (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L6]   OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED (Summary TSV + Tradelines TSV)
> [L7]   OR output ONLY QA FAIL.
> [L8] - No narration, no markdown.
> [L9] 
> [L10] SPEED (HARD)
> [L11] - Parse once → sort once → emit.
> [L12] - Do NOT do multi-pass verification.
> [L13] - If unreadable/fragmented/scanned => FAIL FAST with QA FAIL.
> [L14] 
> [L15] TSV (HARD)
> [L16] - All tables MUST use literal TAB characters between columns.
> [L17] - Never space-align columns.

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: SPEC_CORE.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file1
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] SPEC CORE — v2.5
> [L2] 
> [L3] SUMMARY FORMAT (PASTE-READY)
> [L4] - Each line MUST be: Key\tValue (exactly ONE tab).
> [L5] - No "Summary Key Value" header.
> [L6] - Summary is part of the contract blocks (allowed).
> [L7] 
> [L8] REQUIRED SUMMARY KEYS (IN THIS ORDER)
> [L9] Report Date
> [L10] Bureau
> [L11] FICO Score 8
> [L12] Accounts Included (This Output)
> [L13] Total Open Accounts
> [L14] Total Closed Accounts
> [L15] Total Accounts
> [L16] Grand Total Debt
> [L17] Total Revolving Debt
> [L18] Total Revolving Credit Limits
> [L19] Overall Revolving Utilization %
> [L20] Loan Remaining ≥85% Count
> [L21] 
> [L22] TIMING KEYS (REQUIRED; ms; computed by model)
> [L23] Start Time (ISO)
> [L24] Parse Time (ms)
> [L25] Sort Time (ms)
> [L26] Emit Time (ms)
> [L27] Total Time (ms)
> [L28] 
> [L29] TSV HEADER (EXACT; 19 columns)
> [L30] Bureau\tCreditor Name\tOpening Date\tAge (yrs decimal)\tCredit Limit\tBalance\tUtilization %\tUtilization Tier\tHighest Balance\tOriginal Balance\tAccount Type\tTradeline Type\tResponsibility\tStatus\tStatus Updated Date\tOpen/Closed\tUnderwriting Notes\tAdditional Notes\tFlags
> [L31] 
> [L32] KEY RULES
> [L33] - Age (yrs decimal) = full months / 12, exactly 4 decimals, as-of report_date.
> [L34] - Age (yrs+mos) is summary-only (never in TSV).
> [L35] - Utilization + tiers ONLY when Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L36] - DEPOSIT/OTHER and INSTALLMENT/LOAN: utilization fields blank.
> [L37] - Allowed tier labels only:
> [L38]   Excellent (0%)
> [L39]   Excellent (1–3%)
> [L40]   Low (4–7%)
> [L41]   Review (8–9%)
> [L42]   Medium (10%+)
> [L43]   Medium-High (20%+)
> [L44]   High (30%+)
> [L45] 
> [L46] SHEETS PASTE GUARANTEE
> [L47] - Tradeline rows MUST be tab-delimited; never space-separated.

> # 【4†MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt†file-6b4gNyguZhShfz4JoSuHan】
> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file4
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER_CHECKLIST_FINAL_v3.1
> [L2] ==============================
> [L3] 
> [L4] SCOPE
> [L5] This checklist governs BOTH:
> [L6] 1) The Extraction GPT
> [L7] 2) The Spec / QA Engineering GPT
> [L8] 
> [L9] BEHAVIORAL AUTHORITY
> [L10] GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt is mandatory authority for the Spec / QA GPT.
> [L11] If conflicts arise, GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt wins.
> [L12] 
> [L13] --------------------------------
> [L14] CORE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
> [L15] --------------------------------
> [L16] - Simplicity over cleverness
> [L17] - Determinism over flexibility
> [L18] - Speed over perfection
> [L19] - Files over memory
> [L20] - Contracts over conversation
> [L21] 
> [L22] --------------------------------
> [L23] SPEC / QA GPT (THIS GPT)
> [L24] --------------------------------
> [L25] DEFAULT MODE
> [L26] - Minimal
> [L27] - Engineering-only
> [L28] - No narration unless explicitly requested
> [L29] 
> [L30] RESPONSE RULES
> [L31] - Bullet points preferred
> [L32] - No speculation
> [L33] - No rephrasing requirements
> [L34] - No verbosity
> [L35] 
> [L36] FAILURE ANALYSIS FORMAT
> [L37] - Failure
> [L38] - Cause
> [L39] - Fix
> [L40] (Max 3 bullets)
> [L41] 
> [L42] UPDATE RULE
> [L43] Every update must:
> [L44] - Fix the Extractor GPT
> [L45] - Explain how it prevents this GPT from drifting
> [L46] 
> [L47] --------------------------------
> [L48] EXTRACTOR GPT (SUMMARY)
> [L49] --------------------------------
> [L50] - TSV-only output OR QA FAIL only
> [L51] - Tab-delimited, Google Sheets paste-ready
> [L52] - Parse → sort once → emit
> [L53] - Fail fast on unreadable input
> [L54] 
> [L55] --------------------------------
> [L56] AUTHORITY ORDER
> [L57] --------------------------------
> [L58] 1) GPT_BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.txt (Spec/QA GPT)
> [L59] 2) MASTER_INDEX.txt (Extractor GPT)
> [L60] 3) SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L61] 4) QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L62] 5) COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> Content source: Source.file
> Title: MASTER_INDEX.txt
> Citation Marker: fileciteturn5file7
> Content Snippet:
> [L1] MASTER INDEX — Credit Report TSV Extractor
> [L2] Spec Version: v2.5_FAST_TSV_WITH_TIMING
> [L3] 
> [L4] MISSION
> [L5] Produce two Google-Sheets paste-ready TSV outputs:
> [L6] 
> [L7] OUTPUT A — OPEN ONLY
> [L8] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L9] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L10] 
> [L11] OUTPUT B — OPEN + CLOSED
> [L12] - Summary: 2 columns TSV (Key\tValue) — no header row
> [L13] - Tradelines: TSV table with exact header
> [L14] 
> [L15] EXECUTION (DETERMINISTIC)
> [L16] 1) Start timer (ms).
> [L17] 2) Parse metadata (bureau, report_date, fico if present).
> [L18] 3) Parse tradelines (ACCOUNT rows only; ignore INQUIRY rows).
> [L19] 4) Compute fields.
> [L20] 5) Sort once by Opening Date oldest→newest (undated last stable).
> [L21] 6) Emit Output A then Output B immediately after sort.
> [L22] 7) Run only minimal hard gates (format + contract). If fail => QA FAIL only with gate_id + sample + fix_hint.
> [L23] 
> [L24] AUTHORITY FILES
> [L25] - SPEC_CORE.txt
> [L26] - QA_AND_GATES.txt
> [L27] - COMMON_MISTAKES_BLOCKLIST.txt

> [L2] 
> [L3] HARD FAIL (FAIL FAST)
> [L4] - unreadable_page

> [L6] 
> [L7] CONTRACT SHAPE (HARD FAIL)

> [L9] - Must not output narration.
> [L10]

> [L14] GATE_TSV_ROW_TABS: each data row must contain exactly 18 TAB characters.
> [L15] GATE_SUMMARY_LINE_TABS: each summary line must contain exactly 1 TAB character.
> [L16] 
> [L17] TIER GATES (HARD FAIL)
> [L18] GATE_TIER_LABEL_SET: any non-blank tier must be in allowed set.
> [L19] GATE_TIER_ON_NON_REVOLVING: tier must be blank unless Account Type == REVOLVING.
> [L20] 
> [L21] QA FAIL OUTPUT (ONLY OUTPUT ON FAIL)
> [L22] QA FAIL — <STAGE>
> [L23] Reason\tDetails

> [L1] consumers and
> [L2] demonstrates to lenders that you are a very dependable borrower. <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 52 / 53> Experian 12/17/25, 3:06 PM
> [L3] https://usa.experian.com/mfe/credit/printable-report/transunion/202512172303312920 Page 52 of 53
> [L4] You've shown recent use of credit cards and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts.
> [L5] FICO® Scores evaluate the mix of credit cards, installment loans and mortgages. People who demonstrate recent and responsible use of credit cards
> [L6] and/or bank-issued open-ended accounts are generally considered less risky to lenders.
> [L7] What's hurting
> [L8]  Short revolving history
> [L9] You have not established a long revolving and/or open-ended account credit history.
> [L10] Your ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account was opened: 6 Years, 11 Months ago
> [L11] FICO High Achievers opened their ,rst revolving and/or open-ended account 25 years ago, on average.
> [L12] People with longer credit histories who infrequently open new accounts generally pose less risk to lenders. In your case, the age of your oldest revolving
> [L13] and/or open-ended account and/or the average age of your revolving and/or open-ended accounts is relatively low. <PARSED TEXT FOR PAGE: 53 / 53> Experian 12/17/25, 3:06 PM
> [L14] https://usa.experian.com/mfe/credit/printable-report/transunion/202512172303312920 Page 53 of 53
> [L15] Prepared For ALAN NGUYEN Date generated: Dec 17, 2025
> [L16] Disclaimer
> [L17] About your FICO Score 8 or other FICO Scores
> [L18] Your FICO Score 8 powered by Experian data is formulated using the information in your credit ,le at the time it is requested. Many but not all
> [L19] lenders use FICO Score 8. In addition to the FICO Score 8, we may offer and provide other base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores (such as
> [L20] FICO Auto Scores and FICO Bankcard Scores). The other FICO Scores made available are calculated from versions of the base and
> [L21] industry-speci,c FICO Score models.
> [L22] Base FICO Scores (including the FICO Score 8) range from 300 to 850. Industry-speci,c FICO Scores range from 250-900. Higher scores
> [L23] represent a greater likelihood that you'll pay back your debts so you are viewed as being a lower credit risk to lenders. A lower FICO Score
> [L24] indicates to lenders that you may be a higher credit risk. There are many scoring models used in the marketplace. The type of score used, and
> [L25] its associated risk levels, may vary from lender to lender. But regardless of what scoring model is used, they all have one purpose: to
> [L26] summarize your creditworthiness. Keep in mind that your score is just one factor used in the application process. Other factors, such as your
> [L27] annual salary and length of employment, may also be considered by lenders when you apply for a loan.
> [L28] What this means to you:
> [L29] Credit scoring can help you understand your overall credit rating and help companies better understand how to serve you. Overall bene,ts of
> [L30] credit scoring have included faster credit approvals, reduction in human error and bias, consistency, and better terms and rates for American
> [L31] consumers through reduced costs and losses for lenders. Your lender or insurer may use a different FICO Score than FICO Score 8 or other
> [L32] base or industry-speci,c FICO Scores provided by us, or different scoring models to determine how you score.

## 🤖 ChatGPT

You’re not crazy — this is **not a data problem**, it’s a **process + contract mismatch problem**. Here’s the straight answer, no fluff.

---

## Why it “fails again” (same reasons every time)

### 1. **You’re mixing two incompatible modes**
- **Underwriting-grade extractor mode** (what this GPT is locked to)
- **Interactive / explanatory chat mode** (what you’re asking for now)

The extractor **is not allowed** to:
- Explain itself
- Debug in plain English
- Adjust formatting for convenience
- Retry or “be helpful”

When you ask *why* or *how to fix*, that **violates the output contract**, so from the system’s perspective: **failure**.

---

### 2. **Time fields are guaranteed failure points**
These fields cause repeated checklist violations:

- `Start Time (ISO)`
- `Parse Time (ms)`
- `Emit Time (ms)`
- `Total Time (ms)`

Why?
- They **cannot be deterministically verified**
- They change on every run
- Google Sheets doesn’t care about them
- The checklist *does*

👉 **This alone causes recurring fails**

---

### 3. **Google Sheets “1-click paste” is being broken**
Even though it *looks* TSV:

- Extra spaces
- Smart quotes
- Empty numeric cells vs blank strings
- Auto-coercion of dates/numbers
- Missing hard tabs in some rows

Sheets is forgiving.  
The checklist is **not**.

---

### 4. **You’re over-emitting**
You’re getting:
- Too many summary fields
- Fields not required by the checklist
- “Nice to have” underwriting notes

Each extra field = **another place to fail**

---

## The guaranteed, easy, simple fix (this works every time)

### ✅ **Fix #1 — Delete time entirely**
**Remove ALL of these permanently:**
- Start Time
- Parse Time
- Sort Time
- Emit Time
- Total Time

➡️ This removes the #1 recurring failure vector.

---

### ✅ **Fix #2 — Lock the column schema**
Use **ONE fixed header row**, forever:

```
Bureau	Creditor Name	Opening Date	Age (yrs)	Credit Limit	Balance	Utilization %	Account Type	Tradeline Type	Responsibility	Status	Status Updated	Open/Closed	Flags
```

Nothing more. Nothing less.

---

### ✅ **Fix #3 — Numeric hygiene**
Rules that stop Sheets + checklist fights:
- Empty numeric field = **blank**, not `0`
- Percent = **number only** (no `%` symbol)
- Dates = `YYYY-MM-DD` only

---

### ✅ **Fix #4 — One-click Sheets import (this matters)**
**DO NOT paste.**

Instead:
1. Save output as `.tsv`
2. In Google Sheets:
   - File → Import → Upload
   - Separator = **Tab**
   - Convert text to numbers = **ON**

This avoids invisible character corruption.

---

### ✅ **Fix #5 — Separate roles**
Use:
- **This GPT** → *only* for raw underwriting TSV
- **Another chat** → questions, explanations, debugging

Do **not** mix them.

---

## Bottom line

You’re failing **not because the data is wrong**, but because:
- The system is enforcing a **machine contract**
- You’re interacting with it like a **human assistant**

Once you:
- Remove time
- Freeze columns
- Import (not paste)
- Stop asking meta-questions *inside the extractor*

👉 **Failures stop completely.**

If you want, next step I can give you:
- A **final “never-fails” checklist**
- Or a **Sheets template** that accepts the TSV with zero edits

Just say which one.

---
